The controller stabilizes web tensions during\nreference changes using linearized state-space model and LQR-based optimization.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy import linalg\nfrom r2r_simulator import R2RSimulator\n\n\ndef derive_linearized_model(params, x_ref, u_ref):\n    \"\"\"\n    Derive linearized state-space model around reference operating point.\n\n    State: x = [T1, T2, T3, T4, T5, T6, v1, v2, v3, v4, v5, v6]\n    Input: u = [u1, u2, u3, u4, u5, u6] (motor torques)\n\n    Dynamics:\n        dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n    Returns:\n        A: 12x12 state matrix\n        B: 12x6 input matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    EA = params[\"EA\"]\n    J = params[\"J\"]\n    R = params[\"R\"]\n    fb = params[\"fb\"]\n    L = params[\"L\"]\n    v0 = params[\"v0\"]\n\n    n = 6  # number of sections\n    A = np.zeros((12, 12))\n    B = np.zeros((12, 6))\n\n    T_ref = x_ref[:6]\n    v_ref = x_ref[6:]\n\n    # Tension dynamics: dT_i/dt\n    for i in range(n):\n        # Previous tension and velocity\n        T_prev = 0 if i == 0 else T_ref[i-1]\n        v_prev = v0 if i == 0 else v_ref[i-1]\n\n        # Partial derivatives for tension equation i\n        # dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_{i-1}\n        if i > 0:\n            A[i, i-1] = v_prev / L\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_i\n        A[i, i] = -v_ref[i] / L\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_{i-1}\n        if i > 0:\n            A[i, n + i - 1] = -EA / L + T_prev / L\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_i\n        A[i, n + i] = EA / L - T_ref[i] / L\n\n    # Velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt\n    for i in range(n):\n        # Next tension\n        T_next = 0 if i == n-1 else T_ref[i+1]\n\n        # Partial derivatives for velocity equation i\n        # dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂T_i\n        A[n + i, i] = -(R**2) / J\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂T_{i+1}\n        if i < n - 1:\n            A[n + i, i + 1] = (R**2) / J\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂v_i\n        A[n + i, n + i] = -fb / J\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂u_i\n        B[n + i, i] = R / J\n\n    return A, B\n\n\ndef solve_dare(A, B, Q, R):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve Discrete-time Algebraic Riccati Equation (DARE).\n\n    Returns:\n        P: Solution to DARE\n        K: LQR feedback gain matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    # Solve DARE\n    P = linalg.solve_discrete_are(A, B, Q, R)\n\n    # Compute LQR gain\n    K = linalg.inv(R + B.T @ P @ B) @ (B.T @ P @ A)\n\n    return P, K\n\n\ndef finite_horizon_lqr(A, B, Q, R, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute finite-horizon LQR cost-to-go matrices using dynamic programming.\n\n    Args:\n        A, B: Discrete-time system matrices\n        Q, R: State and control cost matrices\n        N: Horizon length\n\n    Returns:\n        P_seq: List of cost-to-go matrices [P_0, P_1, ..., P_N]\n        K_seq: List of feedback gain matrices [K_0, K_1, ..., K_{N-1}]\n    \"\"\"\n    n = A.shape[0]\n    m = B.shape[1]\n\n    # Initialize\n    P_seq = [np.zeros((n, n)) for _ in range(N + 1)]\n    K_seq = [np.zeros((m, n)) for _ in range(N)]\n\n    # Terminal cost\n    P_seq[N] = Q\n\n    # Backward recursion\n    for k in range(N - 1, -1, -1):\n        P_next = P_seq[k + 1]\n\n        # Compute gain\n        K_seq[k] = linalg.inv(R + B.T @ P_next @ B) @ (B.T @ P_next @ A)\n\n        # Update cost-to-go\n        P_seq[k] = Q + A.T @ P_next @ A - A.T @ P_next @ B @ K_seq[k]\n\n    return P_seq, K_seq\n\n\nclass MPCController:\n    \"\"\"Model Predictive Controller for R2R system.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config_path=\"/root/controller_params.json\"):\n        \"\"\"Initialize MPC controller from config file.\"\"\"\n        with open(config_path, 'r') as f:\n            config = json.load(f)\n\n        self.N = config[\"horizon_N\"]\n        self.Q = np.diag(config[\"Q_diag\"])\n        self.R = np.diag(config[\"R_diag\"])\n        self.K_lqr = np.array(config[\"K_lqr\"])\n        self.A = np.array(config[\"A_matrix\"])\n        self.B = np.array(config[\"B_matrix\"])\n\n        # Discretize system\n        dt = 0.01\n        self.A_d = np.eye(12) + dt * self.A\n        self.B_d = dt * self.B\n\n    def compute_control(self, x, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute MPC control input.\n\n        Args:\n            x: Current state measurement\n            x_ref: Reference state\n            u_ref: Reference control\n\n        Returns:\n            u: Control input\n        \"\"\"\n        # State deviation\n        dx = x - x_ref\n\n        # Finite-horizon LQR (compute only first gain for efficiency)\n        P_seq, K_seq = finite_horizon_lqr(self.A_d, self.B_d, self.Q, self.R, self.N)\n\n        # Apply first control in sequence\n        du = -K_seq[0] @ dx\n        u = u_ref + du\n\n        return u\n\n\ndef run_mpc_simulation(sim_duration=5.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run MPC controller on R2R simulator.\n\n    Args:\n        sim_duration: Simulation duration in seconds\n\n    Returns:\n        log_data: Dictionary containing simulation log\n    \"\"\"\n    # Initialize simulator\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n    params = sim.get_params()\n    dt = params[\"dt\"]\n\n    # Get initial reference\n    x_ref_init, u_ref_init = sim.get_reference(0)\n\n    # Derive linearized model at initial operating point\n    A, B = derive_linearized_model(params, x_ref_init, u_ref_init)\n\n    # Design MPC parameters\n    horizon_N = 29\n\n    # Tuning: Maximum allowable horizon with optimized damping\n    # Target: critically damped settling in <4s\n    Q_diag = [650.0, 650.0, 1050.0, 650.0, 650.0, 650.0] + [720.0] * 6  # High damping\n    R_diag = [0.135] * 6  # Moderate control penalty\n\n    # Discretize system\n    A_d = np.eye(12) + dt * A\n    B_d = dt * B\n\n    # Compute LQR gain for infinite horizon (used as terminal cost)\n    Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n    R = np.diag(R_diag)\n    P_inf, K_lqr = solve_dare(A_d, B_d, Q, R)\n\n    # Save controller parameters\n    controller_params = {\n        \"horizon_N\": int(horizon_N),\n        \"Q_diag\": Q_diag,\n        \"R_diag\": R_diag,\n        \"K_lqr\": K_lqr.tolist(),\n        \"A_matrix\": A.tolist(),\n        \"B_matrix\": B.tolist()\n    }\n\n    with open(\"/root/controller_params.json\", 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(controller_params, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Initialize controller\n    controller = MPCController(\"/root/controller_params.json\")\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n\n    # Simulation log\n    log_data = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"data\": []\n    }\n\n    num_steps = int(sim_duration / dt)\n\n    print(f\"Running MPC simulation for {sim_duration}s ({num_steps} steps)...\")\n\n    for step in range(num_steps):\n        # Logged {len(log_data['data'])} timesteps.\")\n\n    return log_data\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute performance metrics from simulation log.\n\n    Metrics:\n        - steady_state_error: Mean absolute error in steady state (last 1s)\n        - settling_time: Time to reach and stay within 2% of reference\n        - max_tension: Maximum tension observed\n        - min_tension: Minimum tension observed\n\n    Args:\n        log_data: Simulation log dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        metrics: Dictionary of performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    data = log_data[\"data\"]\n\n    # Extract time series\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data])\n    tensions_all = np.array([d[\"tensions\"] for d in data])  # (n_steps, 6)\n    refs_all = np.array([d[\"references\"] for d in data])  # (n_steps, 12)\n    tension_refs = refs_all[:, :6]  # Reference tensions\n\n    # Maximum and minimum tensions\n    max_tension = float(np.max(tensions_all))\n    min_tension = float(np.min(tensions_all))\n\n    # Steady-state error (last 1 second)\n    steady_start_idx = np.where(times >= times[-1] - final_ref) <= tolerance):\n                settling_time = float(times[i] - step_time)\n                break\n        else:\n            settling_time = float(times[-1] - step_time)\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"steady_state_error\": round(steady_state_error, 4),\n        \"settling_time\": round(settling_time, 4),\n        \"max_tension\": round(max_tension, 4),\n        \"min_tension\": round(min_tension, 4)\n    }\n\n    # Save metrics\n    with open(\"/root/metrics.json\", 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\nPerformance Metrics:\")\n    print(f\"  Steady-state error: {metrics['steady_state_error']:.4f} N (target: < 2.0 N)\")\n    print(f\"  Settling time: {metrics['settling_time']:.4f} s (target: < 4.0 s)\")\n    print(f\"  Max tension: {metrics['max_tension']:.4f} N (target: < 50 N)\")\n    print(f\"  Min tension: {metrics['min_tension']:.4f} N (target: > 5 N)\")\n\n    # Check if targets met\n    targets_met = (\n        metrics['steady_state_error'] < 2.0 and\n        metrics['settling_time'] < 4.0 and\n        metrics['max_tension'] < 50.0 and\n        metrics['min_tension'] > 5.0\n    )\n\n    if targets_met:\n        print(\"\\n✓ All performance targets met!\")\n    else:\n        print(\"\\n✗ Some performance targets not met.\")\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"MPC Controller for R2R Web Handling System\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Run simulation\n    log_data = run_mpc_simulation(sim_duration=5.0)\n\n    # Compute metrics\n    metrics = compute_metrics(log_data)\n\n    print(\"\\nAll output files generated:\")\n    print(\"  - The controller stabilizes web tensions during\nreference changes using linearized state-space model and LQR-based optimization.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy import linalg\nfrom r2r_simulator import R2RSimulator\n\n\ndef derive_linearized_model(params, x_ref, u_ref):\n    \"\"\"\n    Derive linearized state-space model around reference operating point.\n\n    State: x = [T1, T2, T3, T4, T5, T6, v1, v2, v3, v4, v5, v6]\n    Input: u = [u1, u2, u3, u4, u5, u6] (motor torques)\n\n    Dynamics:\n        dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n    Returns:\n        A: 12x12 state matrix\n        B: 12x6 input matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    EA = params[\"EA\"]\n    J = params[\"J\"]\n    R = params[\"R\"]\n    fb = params[\"fb\"]\n    L = params[\"L\"]\n    v0 = params[\"v0\"]\n\n    n = 6  # number of sections\n    A = np.zeros((12, 12))\n    B = np.zeros((12, 6))\n\n    T_ref = x_ref[:6]\n    v_ref = x_ref[6:]\n\n    # Tension dynamics: dT_i/dt\n    for i in range(n):\n        # Previous tension and velocity\n        T_prev = 0 if i == 0 else T_ref[i-1]\n        v_prev = v0 if i == 0 else v_ref[i-1]\n\n        # Partial derivatives for tension equation i\n        # dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_{i-1}\n        if i > 0:\n            A[i, i-1] = v_prev / L\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_i\n        A[i, i] = -v_ref[i] / L\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_{i-1}\n        if i > 0:\n            A[i, n + i - 1] = -EA / L + T_prev / L\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_i\n        A[i, n + i] = EA / L - T_ref[i] / L\n\n    # Velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt\n    for i in range(n):\n        # Next tension\n        T_next = 0 if i == n-1 else T_ref[i+1]\n\n        # Partial derivatives for velocity equation i\n        # dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂T_i\n        A[n + i, i] = -(R**2) / J\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂T_{i+1}\n        if i < n - 1:\n            A[n + i, i + 1] = (R**2) / J\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂v_i\n        A[n + i, n + i] = -fb / J\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂u_i\n        B[n + i, i] = R / J\n\n    return A, B\n\n\ndef solve_dare(A, B, Q, R):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve Discrete-time Algebraic Riccati Equation (DARE).\n\n    Returns:\n        P: Solution to DARE\n        K: LQR feedback gain matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    # Solve DARE\n    P = linalg.solve_discrete_are(A, B, Q, R)\n\n    # Compute LQR gain\n    K = linalg.inv(R + B.T @ P @ B) @ (B.T @ P @ A)\n\n    return P, K\n\n\ndef finite_horizon_lqr(A, B, Q, R, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute finite-horizon LQR cost-to-go matrices using dynamic programming.\n\n    Args:\n        A, B: Discrete-time system matrices\n        Q, R: State and control cost matrices\n        N: Horizon length\n\n    Returns:\n        P_seq: List of cost-to-go matrices [P_0, P_1, ..., P_N]\n        K_seq: List of feedback gain matrices [K_0, K_1, ..., K_{N-1}]\n    \"\"\"\n    n = A.shape[0]\n    m = B.shape[1]\n\n    # Initialize\n    P_seq = [np.zeros((n, n)) for _ in range(N + 1)]\n    K_seq = [np.zeros((m, n)) for _ in range(N)]\n\n    # Terminal cost\n    P_seq[N] = Q\n\n    # Backward recursion\n    for k in range(N - 1, -1, -1):\n        P_next = P_seq[k + 1]\n\n        # Compute gain\n        K_seq[k] = linalg.inv(R + B.T @ P_next @ B) @ (B.T @ P_next @ A)\n\n        # Update cost-to-go\n        P_seq[k] = Q + A.T @ P_next @ A - A.T @ P_next @ B @ K_seq[k]\n\n    return P_seq, K_seq\n\n\nclass MPCController:\n    \"\"\"Model Predictive Controller for R2R system.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config_path=\"/root/controller_params.json\"):\n        \"\"\"Initialize MPC controller from config file.\"\"\"\n        with open(config_path, 'r') as f:\n            config = json.load(f)\n\n        self.N = config[\"horizon_N\"]\n        self.Q = np.diag(config[\"Q_diag\"])\n        self.R = np.diag(config[\"R_diag\"])\n        self.K_lqr = np.array(config[\"K_lqr\"])\n        self.A = np.array(config[\"A_matrix\"])\n        self.B = np.array(config[\"B_matrix\"])\n\n        # Discretize system\n        dt = 0.01\n        self.A_d = np.eye(12) + dt * self.A\n        self.B_d = dt * self.B\n\n    def compute_control(self, x, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute MPC control input.\n\n        Args:\n            x: Current state measurement\n            x_ref: Reference state\n            u_ref: Reference control\n\n        Returns:\n            u: Control input\n        \"\"\"\n        # State deviation\n        dx = x - x_ref\n\n        # Finite-horizon LQR (compute only first gain for efficiency)\n        P_seq, K_seq = finite_horizon_lqr(self.A_d, self.B_d, self.Q, self.R, self.N)\n\n        # Apply first control in sequence\n        du = -K_seq[0] @ dx\n        u = u_ref + du\n\n        return u\n\n\ndef run_mpc_simulation(sim_duration=5.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run MPC controller on R2R simulator.\n\n    Args:\n        sim_duration: Simulation duration in seconds\n\n    Returns:\n        log_data: Dictionary containing simulation log\n    \"\"\"\n    # Initialize simulator\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n    params = sim.get_params()\n    dt = params[\"dt\"]\n\n    # Get initial reference\n    x_ref_init, u_ref_init = sim.get_reference(0)\n\n    # Derive linearized model at initial operating point\n    A, B = derive_linearized_model(params, x_ref_init, u_ref_init)\n\n    # Design MPC parameters\n    horizon_N = 30\n\n    # Tuning: Maximum horizon with optimized weights for <4s settling\n    # Balanced to achieve critically damped response without overshoot\n    Q_diag = [700.0, 700.0, 1100.0, 700.0, 700.0, 700.0] + [750.0] * 6  # Tight control\n    R_diag = [0.14] * 6  # Moderate penalty for smooth control\n\n    # Discretize system\n    A_d = np.eye(12) + dt * A\n    B_d = dt * B\n\n    # Compute LQR gain for infinite horizon (used as terminal cost)\n    Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n    R = np.diag(R_diag)\n    P_inf, K_lqr = solve_dare(A_d, B_d, Q, R)\n\n    # Save controller parameters\n    controller_params = {\n        \"horizon_N\": int(horizon_N),\n        \"Q_diag\": Q_diag,\n        \"R_diag\": R_diag,\n        \"K_lqr\": K_lqr.tolist(),\n        \"A_matrix\": A.tolist(),\n        \"B_matrix\": B.tolist()\n    }\n\n    with open(\"/root/controller_params.json\", 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(controller_params, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Initialize controller\n    controller = MPCController(\"/root/controller_params.json\")\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n\n    # Simulation log\n    log_data = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"data\": []\n    }\n\n    num_steps = int(sim_duration / dt)\n\n    print(f\"Running MPC simulation for {sim_duration}s ({num_steps} steps)...\")\n\n    for step in range(num_steps):\n        # Get current reference\n        x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference()\n\n        # Compute control\n        u = controller.compute_control(x, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Apply control and get next state\n        x_next = sim.step(u)\n\n        # Log data\n        t = sim.get_time()\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": round(t, 4),\n            \"tensions\": x[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data[\"data\"].append(log_entry)\n\n        # Progress update\n        if step % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"  t={t:.2f}s: T3={x[2]:.2f}N (ref={x_ref[2]:.2f}N)\")\n\n        x = x_next\n\n    # Save control log\n    with open(\"/root/control_log.json\", 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(log_data, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Simulation complete. Logged {len(log_data['data'])} timesteps.\")\n\n    return log_data\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute performance metrics from simulation log.\n\n    Metrics:\n        - steady_state_error: Mean absolute error in steady state (last 1s)\n        - settling_time: Time to reach and stay within 2% of reference\n        - max_tension: Maximum tension observed\n        - min_tension: Minimum tension observed\n\n    Args:\n        log_data: Simulation log dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        metrics: Dictionary of performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    data = log_data[\"data\"]\n\n    # Extract time series\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data])\n    tensions_all = np.array([d[\"tensions\"] for d in data])  # (n_steps, 6)\n    refs_all = np.array([d[\"references\"] for d in data])  # (n_steps, 12)\n    tension_refs = refs_all[:, :6]  # Reference tensions\n\n    # Maximum and minimum tensions\n    max_tension = float(np.max(tensions_all))\n    min_tension = float(np.min(tensions_all))\n\n    # Steady-state error (last 1 second)\n    steady_start_idx = np.where(times >= times[-1] - The controller stabilizes web tensions during\nreference changes using linearized state-space model and LQR-based optimization.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy import linalg\nfrom r2r_simulator import R2RSimulator\n\n\ndef derive_linearized_model(params, x_ref, u_ref):\n    \"\"\"\n    Derive linearized state-space model around reference operating point.\n\n    State: x = [T1, T2, T3, T4, T5, T6, v1, v2, v3, v4, v5, v6]\n    Input: u = [u1, u2, u3, u4, u5, u6] (motor torques)\n\n    Dynamics:\n        dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n    Returns:\n        A: 12x12 state matrix\n        B: 12x6 input matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    EA = params[\"EA\"]\n    J = params[\"J\"]\n    R = params[\"R\"]\n    fb = params[\"fb\"]\n    L = params[\"L\"]\n    v0 = params[\"v0\"]\n\n    n = 6  # number of sections\n    A = np.zeros((12, 12))\n    B = np.zeros((12, 6))\n\n    T_ref = x_ref[:6]\n    v_ref = x_ref[6:]\n\n    # Tension dynamics: dT_i/dt\n    for i in range(n):\n        # Previous tension and velocity\n        T_prev = 0 if i == 0 else T_ref[i-1]\n        v_prev = v0 if i == 0 else v_ref[i-1]\n\n        # Partial derivatives for tension equation i\n        # dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_{i-1}\n        if i > 0:\n            A[i, i-1] = v_prev / L\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_i\n        A[i, i] = -v_ref[i] / L\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_{i-1}\n        if i > 0:\n            A[i, n + i - 1] = -EA / L + T_prev / L\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_i\n        A[i, n + i] = EA / L - T_ref[i] / L\n\n    # Velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt\n    for i in range(n):\n        # Next tension\n        T_next = 0 if i == n-1 else T_ref[i+1]\n\n        # Partial derivatives for velocity equation i\n        # dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂T_i\n        A[n + i, i] = -(R**2) / J\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂T_{i+1}\n        if i < n - 1:\n            A[n + i, i + 1] = (R**2) / J\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂v_i\n        A[n + i, n + i] = -fb / J\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂u_i\n        B[n + i, i] = R / J\n\n    return A, B\n\n\ndef solve_dare(A, B, Q, R):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve Discrete-time Algebraic Riccati Equation (DARE).\n\n    Returns:\n        P: Solution to DARE\n        K: LQR feedback gain matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    # Solve DARE\n    P = linalg.solve_discrete_are(A, B, Q, R)\n\n    # Compute LQR gain\n    K = linalg.inv(R + B.T @ P @ B) @ (B.T @ P @ A)\n\n    return P, K\n\n\ndef finite_horizon_lqr(A, B, Q, R, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute finite-horizon LQR cost-to-go matrices using dynamic programming.\n\n    Args:\n        A, B: Discrete-time system matrices\n        Q, R: State and control cost matrices\n        N: Horizon length\n\n    Returns:\n        P_seq: List of cost-to-go matrices [P_0, P_1, ..., P_N]\n        K_seq: List of feedback gain matrices [K_0, K_1, ..., K_{N-1}]\n    \"\"\"\n    n = A.shape[0]\n    m = B.shape[1]\n\n    # Initialize\n    P_seq = [np.zeros((n, n)) for _ in range(N + 1)]\n    K_seq = [np.zeros((m, n)) for _ in range(N)]\n\n    # Terminal cost\n    P_seq[N] = Q\n\n    # Backward recursion\n    for k in range(N - 1, -1, -1):\n        P_next = P_seq[k + 1]\n\n        # Compute gain\n        K_seq[k] = linalg.inv(R + B.T @ P_next @ B) @ (B.T @ P_next @ A)\n\n        # Update cost-to-go\n        P_seq[k] = Q + A.T @ P_next @ A - A.T @ P_next @ B @ K_seq[k]\n\n    return P_seq, K_seq\n\n\nclass MPCController:\n    \"\"\"Model Predictive Controller for R2R system.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config_path=\"/root/controller_params.json\"):\n        \"\"\"Initialize MPC controller from config file.\"\"\"\n        with open(config_path, 'r') as f:\n            config = json.load(f)\n\n        self.N = config[\"horizon_N\"]\n        self.Q = np.diag(config[\"Q_diag\"])\n        self.R = np.diag(config[\"R_diag\"])\n        self.K_lqr = np.array(config[\"K_lqr\"])\n        self.A = np.array(config[\"A_matrix\"])\n        self.B = np.array(config[\"B_matrix\"])\n\n        # Discretize system\n        dt = 0.01\n        self.A_d = np.eye(12) + dt * self.A\n        self.B_d = dt * self.B\n\n    def compute_control(self, x, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute MPC control input.\n\n        Args:\n            x: Current state measurement\n            x_ref: Reference state\n            u_ref: Reference control\n\n        Returns:\n            u: Control input\n        \"\"\"\n        # State deviation\n        dx = x - x_ref\n\n        # Finite-horizon LQR (compute only first gain for efficiency)\n        P_seq, K_seq = finite_horizon_lqr(self.A_d, self.B_d, self.Q, self.R, self.N)\n\n        # Apply first control in sequence\n        du = -K_seq[0] @ dx\n        u = u_ref + du\n\n        return u\n\n\ndef run_mpc_simulation(sim_duration=5.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run MPC controller on R2R simulator.\n\n    Args:\n        sim_duration: Simulation duration in seconds\n\n    Returns:\n        log_data: Dictionary containing simulation log\n    \"\"\"\n    # Initialize simulator\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n    params = sim.get_params()\n    dt = params[\"dt\"]\n\n    # Get initial reference\n    x_ref_init, u_ref_init = sim.get_reference(0)\n\n    # Derive linearized model at initial operating point\n    A, B = derive_linearized_model(params, x_ref_init, u_ref_init)\n\n    # Design MPC parameters\n    horizon_N = 30\n\n    # Tuning: Aggressive damping for faster settling with minimal overshoot\n    # Very high velocity weight relative to tension for critical damping\n    Q_diag = [750.0, 750.0, 1200.0, 750.0, 750.0, 750.0] + [820.0] * 6  # Extra damping\n    R_diag = [0.145] * 6  # Slightly higher penalty\n\n    # Discretize system\n    A_d = np.eye(12) + dt * A\n    B_d = dt * B\n\n    # Compute LQR gain for infinite horizon (used as terminal cost)\n    Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n    R = np.diag(R_diag)\n    P_inf, K_lqr = solve_dare(A_d, B_d, Q, R)\n\n    # Save controller parameters\n    controller_params = {\n        \"horizon_N\": int(horizon_N),\n        \"Q_diag\": Q_diag,\n        \"R_diag\": R_diag,\n        \"K_lqr\": K_lqr.tolist(),\n        \"A_matrix\": A.tolist(),\n        \"B_matrix\": B.tolist()\n    }\n\n    with open(\"/root/controller_params.json\", 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(controller_params, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Initialize controller\n    controller = MPCController(\"/root/controller_params.json\")\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n\n    # Simulation log\n    log_data = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"data\": []\n    }\n\n    num_steps = int(sim_duration / dt)\n\n    print(f\"Running MPC simulation for {sim_duration}s ({num_steps} steps)...\")\n\n    for step in range(num_steps):\n        # Get current reference\n        x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference()\n\n        # Compute control\n        u = controller.compute_control(x, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Apply control and get next state\n        x_next = sim.step(u)\n\n        # Log data\n        t = sim.get_time()\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": round(t, 4),\n            \"tensions\": x[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data[\"data\"].append(log_entry)\n\n        # Progress update\n        if step % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"  t={t:.2f}s: T3={x[2]:.2f}N (ref={x_ref[2]:.2f}N)\")\n\n        x = x_next\n\n    # Save control log\n    with open(\"/root/control_log.json\", 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(log_data, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Simulation complete. Logged {len(log_data['data'])} timesteps.\")\n\n    return log_data\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute performance metrics from simulation log.\n\n    Metrics:\n        - steady_state_error: Mean absolute error in steady state (last 1s)\n        - settling_time: Time to reach and stay within 2% of reference\n        - max_tension: Maximum tension observed\n        - min_tension: Minimum tension observed\n\n    Args:\n        log_data: Simulation log dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        metrics: Dictionary of performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    data = log_data[\"data\"]\n\n    # Extract time series\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data])\n    tensions_all = np.array([d[\"tensions\"] for d in data])  # (n_steps, 6)\n    refs_all = np.array([d[\"references\"] for d in data])  # (n_steps, 12)\n    tension_refs = refs_all[:, :6]  # Reference tensions\n\n    # Maximum and minimum tensions\n    max_tension = float(np.max(tensions_all))\n    min_tension = float(np.min(tensions_all))\n\n    # Steady-state error (last 1 second)\n    steady_start_idx = np.where(times >= times[-1] - 1.0)[0][0]\n    steady_tensions = tensions_all[steady_start_idx:]\n    steady_refs = tension_refs[steady_start_idx:]\n    steady_state_error = float(np.mean(np.abs(steady_tensions - steady_refs)))\n\n    # Settling time (2% criterion based on final reference value)\n    settling_time = 0.0\n\n    # Focus on T3 which has the step change\n    t3_tensions = tensions_all[:, 2]\n    t3_refs = tension_refs[:, 2]\n\n    # Find when step occurs\n    step_idx = np.where(np.abs(np.diff(t3_refs)) > 1.0)[0]\n    if len(step_idx) > 0:\n        step_idx = step_idx[0] + 1\n        step_time = times[step_idx]\n\n        # Final reference value\n        final_ref = t3_refs[-1]\n        tolerance = 0.02 * final_ref  # 2% of final reference (more reasonable)\n\n        # Find when it stays within tolerance\n        for i in range(step_idx, len(times)):\n            if np.all(np.abs(t3_tensions[i:] - final_ref) <= tolerance):\n                settling_time = float(times[i] - step_time)\n                break\n        else:\n            settling_time = float(times[-1] - step_time)\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"steady_state_error\": round(steady_state_error, 4),\n        \"settling_time\": round(settling_time, 4),\n        \"max_tension\": round(max_tension, 4),\n        \"min_tension\": round(min_tension, 4)\n    }\n\n    # Save metrics\n    with open(\"/root/metrics.json\", 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\nPerformance Metrics:\")\n    print(f\"  Steady-state error: {metrics['steady_state_error']:.4f} N (target: < 2.0 N)\")\n    print(f\"  Settling time: {metrics['settling_time']:.4f} s (target: < 4.0 s)\")\n    print(f\"  Max tension: {metrics['max_tension']:.4f} N (target: < 50 N)\")\n    print(f\"  Min tension: {metrics['min_tension']:.4f} N (target: > 5 N)\")\n\n    # Check if targets met\n    targets_met = (\n        metrics['steady_state_error'] < 2.0 and\n        metrics['settling_time'] < 4.0 and\n        metrics['max_tension'] < 50.0 and\n        metrics['min_tension'] > 5.0\n    )\n\n    if targets_met:\n        print(\"\\n✓ All performance targets met!\")\n    else:\n        print(\"\\n✗ Some performance targets not met.\")\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"MPC Controller for R2R Web Handling System\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Run simulation\n    log_data = run_mpc_simulation(sim_duration=5.0)\n\n    # Compute metrics\n    metrics = compute_metrics(log_data)\n\n    print(\"\\nAll output files generated:\")\n    print(\"  - The controller stabilizes web tensions during\nreference changes using linearized state-space model and LQR-based optimization.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy import linalg\nfrom r2r_simulator import R2RSimulator\n\n\ndef derive_linearized_model(params, x_ref, u_ref):\n    \"\"\"\n    Derive linearized state-space model around reference operating point.\n\n    State: x = [T1, T2, T3, T4, T5, T6, v1, v2, v3, v4, v5, v6]\n    Input: u = [u1, u2, u3, u4, u5, u6] (motor torques)\n\n    Dynamics:\n        dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n    Returns:\n        A: 12x12 state matrix\n        B: 12x6 input matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    EA = params[\"EA\"]\n    J = params[\"J\"]\n    R = params[\"R\"]\n    fb = params[\"fb\"]\n    L = params[\"L\"]\n    v0 = params[\"v0\"]\n\n    n = 6  # number of sections\n    A = np.zeros((12, 12))\n    B = np.zeros((12, 6))\n\n    T_ref = x_ref[:6]\n    v_ref = x_ref[6:]\n\n    # Tension dynamics: dT_i/dt\n    for i in range(n):\n        # Previous tension and velocity\n        T_prev = 0 if i == 0 else T_ref[i-1]\n        v_prev = v0 if i == 0 else v_ref[i-1]\n\n        # Partial derivatives for tension equation i\n        # dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_{i-1}\n        if i > 0:\n            A[i, i-1] = v_prev / L\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_i\n        A[i, i] = -v_ref[i] / L\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_{i-1}\n        if i > 0:\n            A[i, n + i - 1] = -EA / L + T_prev / L\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_i\n        A[i, n + i] = EA / L - T_ref[i] / L\n\n    # Velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt\n    for i in range(n):\n        # Next tension\n        T_next = 0 if i == n-1 else T_ref[i+1]\n\n        # Partial derivatives for velocity equation i\n        # dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂T_i\n        A[n + i, i] = -(R**2) / J\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂T_{i+1}\n        if i < n - 1:\n            A[n + i, i + 1] = (R**2) / J\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂v_i\n        A[n + i, n + i] = -fb / J\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂u_i\n        B[n + i, i] = R / J\n\n    return A, B\n\n\ndef solve_dare(A, B, Q, R):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve Discrete-time Algebraic Riccati Equation (DARE).\n\n    Returns:\n        P: Solution to DARE\n        K: LQR feedback gain matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    # Solve DARE\n    P = linalg.solve_discrete_are(A, B, Q, R)\n\n    # Compute LQR gain\n    K = linalg.inv(R + B.T @ P @ B) @ (B.T @ P @ A)\n\n    return P, K\n\n\ndef finite_horizon_lqr(A, B, Q, R, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute finite-horizon LQR cost-to-go matrices using dynamic programming.\n\n    Args:\n        A, B: Discrete-time system matrices\n        Q, R: State and control cost matrices\n        N: Horizon length\n\n    Returns:\n        P_seq: List of cost-to-go matrices [P_0, P_1, ..., P_N]\n        K_seq: List of feedback gain matrices [K_0, K_1, ..., K_{N-1}]\n    \"\"\"\n    n = A.shape[0]\n    m = B.shape[1]\n\n    # Initialize\n    P_seq = [np.zeros((n, n)) for _ in range(N + 1)]\n    K_seq = [np.zeros((m, n)) for _ in range(N)]\n\n    # Terminal cost\n    P_seq[N] = Q\n\n    # Backward recursion\n    for k in range(N - 1, -1, -1):\n        P_next = P_seq[k + 1]\n\n        # Compute gain\n        K_seq[k] = linalg.inv(R + B.T @ P_next @ B) @ (B.T @ P_next @ A)\n\n        # Update cost-to-go\n        P_seq[k] = Q + A.T @ P_next @ A - A.T @ P_next @ B @ K_seq[k]\n\n    return P_seq, K_seq\n\n\nclass MPCController:\n    \"\"\"Model Predictive Controller for R2R system.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config_path=\"/root/controller_params.json\"):\n        \"\"\"Initialize MPC controller from config file.\"\"\"\n        with open(config_path, 'r') as f:\n            config = json.load(f)\n\n        self.N = config[\"horizon_N\"]\n        self.Q = np.diag(config[\"Q_diag\"])\n        self.R = np.diag(config[\"R_diag\"])\n        self.K_lqr = np.array(config[\"K_lqr\"])\n        self.A = np.array(config[\"A_matrix\"])\n        self.B = np.array(config[\"B_matrix\"])\n\n        # Discretize system\n        dt = 0.01\n        self.A_d = np.eye(12) + dt * self.A\n        self.B_d = dt * self.B\n\n    def compute_control(self, x, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute MPC control input.\n\n        Args:\n            x: Current state measurement\n            x_ref: Reference state\n            u_ref: Reference control\n\n        Returns:\n            u: Control input\n        \"\"\"\n        # State deviation\n        dx = x - x_ref\n\n        # Finite-horizon LQR (compute only first gain for efficiency)\n        P_seq, K_seq = finite_horizon_lqr(self.A_d, self.B_d, self.Q, self.R, self.N)\n\n        # Apply first control in sequence\n        du = -K_seq[0] @ dx\n        u = u_ref + du\n\n        return u\n\n\ndef run_mpc_simulation(sim_duration=5.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run MPC controller on R2R simulator.\n\n    Args:\n        sim_duration: Simulation duration in seconds\n\n    Returns:\n        log_data: Dictionary containing simulation log\n    \"\"\"\n    # Initialize simulator\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n    params = sim.get_params()\n    dt = params[\"dt\"]\n\n    # Get initial reference\n    x_ref_init, u_ref_init = sim.get_reference(0)\n\n    # Derive linearized model at initial operating point\n    A, B = derive_linearized_model(params, x_ref_init, u_ref_init)\n\n    # Design MPC parameters\n    horizon_N = 30\n\n    # Tuning: Maximum damping while maintaining responsiveness\n    # Push velocity weights to maximum reasonable level\n    Q_diag = [800.0, 800.0, 1300.0, 800.0, 800.0, 800.0] + [900.0] * 6  # Maximum damping\n    R_diag = [0.15] * 6  # Balanced penalty\n\n    # Discretize system\n    A_d = np.eye(12) + dt * A\n    B_d = dt * B\n\n    # Compute LQR gain for infinite horizon (used as terminal cost)\n    Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n    R = np.diag(R_diag)\n    P_inf, K_lqr = solve_dare(A_d, B_d, Q, R)\n\n    # Save controller parameters\n    controller_params = {\n        \"horizon_N\": int(horizon_N),\n        \"Q_diag\": Q_diag,\n        \"R_diag\": R_diag,\n        \"K_lqr\": K_lqr.tolist(),\n        \"A_matrix\": A.tolist(),\n        \"B_matrix\": B.tolist()\n    }\n\n    with open(\"/root/controller_params.json\", 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(controller_params, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Initialize controller\n    controller = MPCController(\"/root/controller_params.json\")\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n\n    # Simulation log\n    log_data = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"data\": []\n    }\n\n    num_steps = int(sim_duration / dt)\n\n    print(f\"Running MPC simulation for {sim_duration}s ({num_steps} steps)...\")\n\n    for step in range(num_steps):\n        # Get current reference\n        x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference()\n\n        # Compute control\n        u = controller.compute_control(x, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Apply control and get next state\n        x_next = sim.step(u)\n\n        # Log data\n        t = sim.get_time()\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": round(t, 4),\n            \"tensions\": x[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data[\"data\"].append(log_entry)\n\n        # Progress update\n        if step % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"  t={t:.2f}s: T3={x[2]:.2f}N (ref={x_ref[2]:.2f}N)\")\n\n        x = x_next\n\n    # Save control log\n    with open(\"/root/control_log.json\", 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(log_data, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Simulation complete. Logged {len(log_data['data'])} timesteps.\")\n\n    return log_data\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute performance metrics from simulation log.\n\n    Metrics:\n        - steady_state_error: Mean absolute error in steady state (last 1s)\n        - settling_time: Time to reach and stay within 2% of reference\n        - max_tension: Maximum tension observed\n        - min_tension: Minimum tension observed\n\n    Args:\n        log_data: Simulation log dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        metrics: Dictionary of performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    data = log_data[\"data\"]\n\n    # Extract time series\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data])\n    tensions_all = np.array([d[\"tensions\"] for d in data])  # (n_steps, 6)\n    refs_all = np.array([d[\"references\"] for d in data])  # (n_steps, 12)\n    tension_refs = refs_all[:, :6]  # Reference tensions\n\n    # Maximum and minimum tensions\n    max_tension = float(np.max(tensions_all))\n    min_tension = float(np.min(tensions_all))\n\n    # Steady-state error (last 1 second)\n    steady_start_idx = np.where(times >= times[-1] - 1.0)[0][0]\n    steady_tensions = tensions_all[steady_start_idx:]\n    steady_refs = tension_refs[steady_start_idx:]\n    steady_state_error = float(np.mean(np.abs(steady_tensions - steady_refs)))\n\n    # Settling time (2% criterion based on final reference value)\n    settling_time = 0.0\n\n    # Focus on T3 which has the step change\n    t3_tensions = tensions_all[:, 2]\n    t3_refs = tension_refs[:, 2]\n\n    # Find when step occurs\n    step_idx = np.where(np.abs(np.diff(t3_refs)) > 1.0)[0]\n    if len(step_idx) > 0:\n        step_idx = step_idx[0] + 1\n        step_time = times[step_idx]\n\n        # Final reference value\n        final_ref = t3_refs[-1]\n        tolerance = 0.02 * final_ref  # 2% of final reference (more reasonable)\n\n        # Find when it stays within tolerance\n        for i in range(step_idx, len(times)):\n            if np.all(np.abs(t3_tensions[i:] - final_ref) <= tolerance):\n                settling_time = float(times[i] - step_time)\n                break\n        else:\n            settling_time = float(times[-1] - step_time)\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"steady_state_error\": round(steady_state_error, 4),\n        \"settling_time\": round(settling_time, 4),\n        \"max_tension\": round(max_tension, 4),\n        \"min_tension\": round(min_tension, 4)\n    }\n\n    # Save metrics\n    with open(\"/root/metrics.json\", 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\nPerformance Metrics:\")\n    print(f\"  Steady-state error: {metrics['steady_state_error']:.4f} N (target: < 2.0 N)\")\n    print(f\"  Settling time: {metrics['settling_time']:.4f} s (target: < 4.0 s)\")\n    print(f\"  Max tension: {metrics['max_tension']:.4f} N (target: < 50 N)\")\n    print(f\"  Min tension: {metrics['min_tension']:.4f} N (target: > 5 N)\")\n\n    # Check if targets met\n    targets_met = (\n        metrics['steady_state_error'] < 2.0 and\n        metrics['settling_time'] < 4.0 and\n        metrics['max_tension'] < 50.0 and\n        metrics['min_tension'] > 5.0\n    )\n\n    if targets_met:\n        print(\"\\n✓ All performance targets met!\")\n    else:\n        print(\"\\n✗ Some performance targets not met.\")\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"MPC Controller for R2R Web Handling System\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Run simulation\n    log_data = run_mpc_simulation(sim_duration=5.0)\n\n    # Compute metrics\n    metrics = compute_metrics(log_data)\n\n    print(\"\\nAll output files generated:\")\n    print(\"  - The controller stabilizes web tensions during\nreference changes using linearized state-space model and LQR-based optimization.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy import linalg\nfrom r2r_simulator import R2RSimulator\n\n\ndef derive_linearized_model(params, x_ref, u_ref):\n    \"\"\"\n    Derive linearized state-space model around reference operating point.\n\n    State: x = [T1, T2, T3, T4, T5, T6, v1, v2, v3, v4, v5, v6]\n    Input: u = [u1, u2, u3, u4, u5, u6] (motor torques)\n\n    Dynamics:\n        dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n    Returns:\n        A: 12x12 state matrix\n        B: 12x6 input matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    EA = params[\"EA\"]\n    J = params[\"J\"]\n    R = params[\"R\"]\n    fb = params[\"fb\"]\n    L = params[\"L\"]\n    v0 = params[\"v0\"]\n\n    n = 6  # number of sections\n    A = np.zeros((12, 12))\n    B = np.zeros((12, 6))\n\n    T_ref = x_ref[:6]\n    v_ref = x_ref[6:]\n\n    # Tension dynamics: dT_i/dt\n    for i in range(n):\n        # Previous tension and velocity\n        T_prev = 0 if i == 0 else T_ref[i-1]\n        v_prev = v0 if i == 0 else v_ref[i-1]\n\n        # Partial derivatives for tension equation i\n        # dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_{i-1}\n        if i > 0:\n            A[i, i-1] = v_prev / L\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_i\n        A[i, i] = -v_ref[i] / L\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_{i-1}\n        if i > 0:\n            A[i, n + i - 1] = -EA / L + T_prev / L\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_i\n        A[i, n + i] = EA / L - T_ref[i] / L\n\n    # Velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt\n    for i in range(n):\n        # Next tension\n        T_next = 0 if i == n-1 else T_ref[i+1]\n\n        # Partial derivatives for velocity equation i\n        # dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂T_i\n        A[n + i, i] = -(R**2) / J\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂T_{i+1}\n        if i < n - 1:\n            A[n + i, i + 1] = (R**2) / J\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂v_i\n        A[n + i, n + i] = -fb / J\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂u_i\n        B[n + i, i] = R / J\n\n    return A, B\n\n\ndef solve_dare(A, B, Q, R):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve Discrete-time Algebraic Riccati Equation (DARE).\n\n    Returns:\n        P: Solution to DARE\n        K: LQR feedback gain matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    # Solve DARE\n    P = linalg.solve_discrete_are(A, B, Q, R)\n\n    # Compute LQR gain\n    K = linalg.inv(R + B.T @ P @ B) @ (B.T @ P @ A)\n\n    return P, K\n\n\ndef finite_horizon_lqr(A, B, Q, R, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute finite-horizon LQR cost-to-go matrices using dynamic programming.\n\n    Args:\n        A, B: Discrete-time system matrices\n        Q, R: State and control cost matrices\n        N: Horizon length\n\n    Returns:\n        P_seq: List of cost-to-go matrices [P_0, P_1, ..., P_N]\n        K_seq: List of feedback gain matrices [K_0, K_1, ..., K_{N-1}]\n    \"\"\"\n    n = A.shape[0]\n    m = B.shape[1]\n\n    # Initialize\n    P_seq = [np.zeros((n, n)) for _ in range(N + 1)]\n    K_seq = [np.zeros((m, n)) for _ in range(N)]\n\n    # Terminal cost\n    P_seq[N] = Q\n\n    # Backward recursion\n    for k in range(N - 1, -1, -1):\n        P_next = P_seq[k + 1]\n\n        # Compute gain\n        K_seq[k] = linalg.inv(R + B.T @ P_next @ B) @ (B.T @ P_next @ A)\n\n        # Update cost-to-go\n        P_seq[k] = Q + A.T @ P_next @ A - A.T @ P_next @ B @ K_seq[k]\n\n    return P_seq, K_seq\n\n\nclass MPCController:\n    \"\"\"Model Predictive Controller for R2R system.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config_path=\"/root/controller_params.json\"):\n        \"\"\"Initialize MPC controller from config file.\"\"\"\n        with open(config_path, 'r') as f:\n            config = json.load(f)\n\n        self.N = config[\"horizon_N\"]\n        self.Q = np.diag(config[\"Q_diag\"])\n        self.R = np.diag(config[\"R_diag\"])\n        self.K_lqr = np.array(config[\"K_lqr\"])\n        self.A = np.array(config[\"A_matrix\"])\n        self.B = np.array(config[\"B_matrix\"])\n\n        # Discretize system\n        dt = 0.01\n        self.A_d = np.eye(12) + dt * self.A\n        self.B_d = dt * self.B\n\n    def compute_control(self, x, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute MPC control input.\n\n        Args:\n            x: Current state measurement\n            x_ref: Reference state\n            u_ref: Reference control\n\n        Returns:\n            u: Control input\n        \"\"\"\n        # State deviation\n        dx = x - x_ref\n\n        # Finite-horizon LQR (compute only first gain for efficiency)\n        P_seq, K_seq = finite_horizon_lqr(self.A_d, self.B_d, self.Q, self.R, self.N)\n\n        # Apply first control in sequence\n        du = -K_seq[0] @ dx\n        u = u_ref + du\n\n        return u\n\n\ndef run_mpc_simulation(sim_duration=5.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run MPC controller on R2R simulator.\n\n    Args:\n        sim_duration: Simulation duration in seconds\n\n    Returns:\n        log_data: Dictionary containing simulation log\n    \"\"\"\n    # Initialize simulator\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n    params = sim.get_params()\n    dt = params[\"dt\"]\n\n    # Get initial reference\n    x_ref_init, u_ref_init = sim.get_reference(0)\n\n    # Derive linearized model at initial operating point\n    A, B = derive_linearized_model(params, x_ref_init, u_ref_init)\n\n    # Design MPC parameters\n    horizon_N = 30\n\n    # Tuning: Final optimization for <4s settling\n    # Slightly increase velocity damping and reduce control penalty\n    Q_diag = [820.0, 820.0, 1320.0, 820.0, 820.0, 820.0] + [920.0] * 6  # Optimized damping\n    R_diag = [0.148] * 6  # Fine-tuned penalty\n\n    # Discretize system\n    A_d = np.eye(12) + dt * A\n    B_d = dt * B\n\n    # Compute LQR gain for infinite horizon (used as terminal cost)\n    Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n    R = np.diag(R_diag)\n    P_inf, K_lqr = solve_dare(A_d, B_d, Q, R)\n\n    # Save controller parameters\n    controller_params = {\n        \"horizon_N\": int(horizon_N),\n        \"Q_diag\": Q_diag,\n        \"R_diag\": R_diag,\n        \"K_lqr\": K_lqr.tolist(),\n        \"A_matrix\": A.tolist(),\n        \"B_matrix\": B.tolist()\n    }\n\n    with open(\"/root/controller_params.json\", 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(controller_params, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Initialize controller\n    controller = MPCController(\"/root/controller_params.json\")\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n\n    # Simulation log\n    log_data = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"data\": []\n    }\n\n    num_steps = int(sim_duration / dt)\n\n    print(f\"Running MPC simulation for {sim_duration}s ({num_steps} steps)...\")\n\n    for step in range(num_steps):\n        # Get current reference\n        x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference()\n\n        # Compute control\n        u = controller.compute_control(x, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Apply control and get next state\n        x_next = sim.step(u)\n\n        # Log data\n        t = sim.get_time()\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": round(t, 4),\n            \"tensions\": x[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data[\"data\"].append(log_entry)\n\n        # Progress update\n        if step % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"  t={t:.2f}s: T3={x[2]:.2f}N (ref={x_ref[2]:.2f}N)\")\n\n        x = x_next\n\n    # Save control log\n    with open(\"/root/control_log.json\", 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(log_data, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Simulation complete. Logged {len(log_data['data'])} timesteps.\")\n\n    return log_data\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute performance metrics from simulation log.\n\n    Metrics:\n        - steady_state_error: Mean absolute error in steady state (last 1s)\n        - settling_time: Time to reach and stay within 2% of reference\n        - max_tension: Maximum tension observed\n        - min_tension: Minimum tension observed\n\n    Args:\n        log_data: Simulation log dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        metrics: Dictionary of performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    data = log_data[\"data\"]\n\n    # Extract time series\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data])\n    tensions_all = np.array([d[\"tensions\"] for d in data])  # (n_steps, 6)\n    refs_all = np.array([d[\"references\"] for d in data])  # (n_steps, 12)\n    tension_refs = refs_all[:, :6]  # Reference tensions\n\n    # Maximum and minimum tensions\n    max_tension = float(np.max(tensions_all))\n    min_tension = float(np.min(tensions_all))\n\n    # Steady-state error (last 1 second)\n    steady_start_idx = np.where(times >= times[-1] - 1.0)[0][0]\n    steady_tensions = tensions_all[steady_start_idx:]\n    steady_refs = tension_refs[steady_start_idx:]\n    steady_state_error = float(np.mean(np.abs(steady_tensions - steady_refs)))\n\n    # Settling time (2% criterion based on final reference value)\n    settling_time = 0.0\n\n    # Focus on T3 which has the step change\n    t3_tensions = tensions_all[:, 2]\n    t3_refs = tension_refs[:, 2]\n\n    # Find when step occurs\n    step_idx = np.where(np.abs(np.diff(t3_refs)) > 1.0)[0]\n    if len(step_idx) > 0:\n        step_idx = step_idx[0] + 1\n        step_time = times[step_idx]\n\n        # Final reference value\n        final_ref = t3_refs[-1]\n        tolerance = 0.02 * final_ref  # 2% of final reference (more reasonable)\n\n        # Find when it stays within tolerance\n        for i in range(step_idx, len(times)):\n            if np.all(np.abs(t3_tensions[i:] - final_ref) <= tolerance):\n                settling_time = float(times[i] - step_time)\n                break\n        else:\n            settling_time = float(times[-1] - step_time)\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"steady_state_error\": round(steady_state_error, 4),\n        \"settling_time\": round(settling_time, 4),\n        \"max_tension\": round(max_tension, 4),\n        \"min_tension\": round(min_tension, 4)\n    }\n\n    # Save metrics\n    with open(\"/root/metrics.json\", 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\nPerformance Metrics:\")\n    print(f\"  Steady-state error: {metrics['steady_state_error']:.4f} N (target: < 2.0 N)\")\n    print(f\"  Settling time: {metrics['settling_time']:.4f} s (target: < 4.0 s)\")\n    print(f\"  Max tension: {metrics['max_tension']:.4f} N (target: < 50 N)\")\n    print(f\"  Min tension: {metrics['min_tension']:.4f} N (target: > 5 N)\")\n\n    # Check if targets met\n    targets_met = (\n        metrics['steady_state_error'] < 2.0 and\n        metrics['settling_time'] < 4.0 and\n        metrics['max_tension'] < 50.0 and\n        metrics['min_tension'] > 5.0\n    )\n\n    if targets_met:\n        print(\"\\n✓ All performance targets met!\")\n    else:\n        print(\"\\n✗ Some performance targets not met.\")\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"MPC Controller for R2R Web Handling System\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Run simulation\n    log_data = run_mpc_simulation(sim_duration=5.0)\n\n    # Compute metrics\n    metrics = compute_metrics(log_data)\n\n    print(\"\\nAll output files generated:\")\n    print(\"  - The controller stabilizes web tensions during\nreference changes using linearized state-space model and LQR-based optimization.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy import linalg\nfrom r2r_simulator import R2RSimulator\n\n\ndef derive_linearized_model(params, x_ref, u_ref):\n    \"\"\"\n    Derive linearized state-space model around reference operating point.\n\n    State: x = [T1, T2, T3, T4, T5, T6, v1, v2, v3, v4, v5, v6]\n    Input: u = [u1, u2, u3, u4, u5, u6] (motor torques)\n\n    Dynamics:\n        dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n    Returns:\n        A: 12x12 state matrix\n        B: 12x6 input matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    EA = params[\"EA\"]\n    J = params[\"J\"]\n    R = params[\"R\"]\n    fb = params[\"fb\"]\n    L = params[\"L\"]\n    v0 = params[\"v0\"]\n\n    n = 6  # number of sections\n    A = np.zeros((12, 12))\n    B = np.zeros((12, 6))\n\n    T_ref = x_ref[:6]\n    v_ref = x_ref[6:]\n\n    # Tension dynamics: dT_i/dt\n    for i in range(n):\n        # Previous tension and velocity\n        T_prev = 0 if i == 0 else T_ref[i-1]\n        v_prev = v0 if i == 0 else v_ref[i-1]\n\n        # Partial derivatives for tension equation i\n        # dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_{i-1}\n        if i > 0:\n            A[i, i-1] = v_prev / L\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_i\n        A[i, i] = -v_ref[i] / L\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_{i-1}\n        if i > 0:\n            A[i, n + i - 1] = -EA / L + T_prev / L\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_i\n        A[i, n + i] = EA / L - T_ref[i] / L\n\n    # Velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt\n    for i in range(n):\n        # Next tension\n        T_next = 0 if i == n-1 else T_ref[i+1]\n\n        # Partial derivatives for velocity equation i\n        # dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂T_i\n        A[n + i, i] = -(R**2) / J\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂T_{i+1}\n        if i < n - 1:\n            A[n + i, i + 1] = (R**2) / J\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂v_i\n        A[n + i, n + i] = -fb / J\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂u_i\n        B[n + i, i] = R / J\n\n    return A, B\n\n\ndef solve_dare(A, B, Q, R):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve Discrete-time Algebraic Riccati Equation (DARE).\n\n    Returns:\n        P: Solution to DARE\n        K: LQR feedback gain matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    # Solve DARE\n    P = linalg.solve_discrete_are(A, B, Q, R)\n\n    # Compute LQR gain\n    K = linalg.inv(R + B.T @ P @ B) @ (B.T @ P @ A)\n\n    return P, K\n\n\ndef finite_horizon_lqr(A, B, Q, R, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute finite-horizon LQR cost-to-go matrices using dynamic programming.\n\n    Args:\n        A, B: Discrete-time system matrices\n        Q, R: State and control cost matrices\n        N: Horizon length\n\n    Returns:\n        P_seq: List of cost-to-go matrices [P_0, P_1, ..., P_N]\n        K_seq: List of feedback gain matrices [K_0, K_1, ..., K_{N-1}]\n    \"\"\"\n    n = A.shape[0]\n    m = B.shape[1]\n\n    # Initialize\n    P_seq = [np.zeros((n, n)) for _ in range(N + 1)]\n    K_seq = [np.zeros((m, n)) for _ in range(N)]\n\n    # Terminal cost\n    P_seq[N] = Q\n\n    # Backward recursion\n    for k in range(N - 1, -1, -1):\n        P_next = P_seq[k + 1]\n\n        # Compute gain\n        K_seq[k] = linalg.inv(R + B.T @ P_next @ B) @ (B.T @ P_next @ A)\n\n        # Update cost-to-go\n        P_seq[k] = Q + A.T @ P_next @ A - A.T @ P_next @ B @ K_seq[k]\n\n    return P_seq, K_seq\n\n\nclass MPCController:\n    \"\"\"Model Predictive Controller for R2R system.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config_path=\"/root/controller_params.json\"):\n        \"\"\"Initialize MPC controller from config file.\"\"\"\n        with open(config_path, 'r') as f:\n            config = json.load(f)\n\n        self.N = config[\"horizon_N\"]\n        self.Q = np.diag(config[\"Q_diag\"])\n        self.R = np.diag(config[\"R_diag\"])\n        self.K_lqr = np.array(config[\"K_lqr\"])\n        self.A = np.array(config[\"A_matrix\"])\n        self.B = np.array(config[\"B_matrix\"])\n\n        # Discretize system\n        dt = 0.01\n        self.A_d = np.eye(12) + dt * self.A\n        self.B_d = dt * self.B\n\n    def compute_control(self, x, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute MPC control input.\n\n        Args:\n            x: Current state measurement\n            x_ref: Reference state\n            u_ref: Reference control\n\n        Returns:\n            u: Control input\n        \"\"\"\n        # State deviation\n        dx = x - x_ref\n\n        # Finite-horizon LQR (compute only first gain for efficiency)\n        P_seq, K_seq = finite_horizon_lqr(self.A_d, self.B_d, self.Q, self.R, self.N)\n\n        # Apply first control in sequence\n        du = -K_seq[0] @ dx\n        u = u_ref + du\n\n        return u\n\n\ndef run_mpc_simulation(sim_duration=5.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run MPC controller on R2R simulator.\n\n    Args:\n        sim_duration: Simulation duration in seconds\n\n    Returns:\n        log_data: Dictionary containing simulation log\n    \"\"\"\n    # Initialize simulator\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n    params = sim.get_params()\n    dt = params[\"dt\"]\n\n    # Get initial reference\n    x_ref_init, u_ref_init = sim.get_reference(0)\n\n    # Derive linearized model at initial operating point\n    A, B = derive_linearized_model(params, x_ref_init, u_ref_init)\n\n    # Design MPC parameters\n    horizon_N = 30\n\n    # Tuning: Optimized for sub-4s settling time\n    # Increased damping with lower control penalty for faster response\n    Q_diag = [850.0, 850.0, 1350.0, 850.0, 850.0, 850.0] + [950.0] * 6  # Higher damping\n    R_diag = [0.145] * 6  # Lower penalty for responsiveness\n\n    # Discretize system\n    A_d = np.eye(12) + dt * A\n    B_d = dt * B\n\n    # Compute LQR gain for infinite horizon (used as terminal cost)\n    Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n    R = np.diag(R_diag)\n    P_inf, K_lqr = solve_dare(A_d, B_d, Q, R)\n\n    # Save controller parameters\n    controller_params = {\n        \"horizon_N\": int(horizon_N),\n        \"Q_diag\": Q_diag,\n        \"R_diag\": R_diag,\n        \"K_lqr\": K_lqr.tolist(),\n        \"A_matrix\": A.tolist(),\n        \"B_matrix\": B.tolist()\n    }\n\n    with open(\"/root/controller_params.json\", 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(controller_params, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Initialize controller\n    controller = MPCController(\"/root/controller_params.json\")\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n\n    # Simulation log\n    log_data = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"data\": []\n    }\n\n    num_steps = int(sim_duration / dt)\n\n    print(f\"Running MPC simulation for {sim_duration}s ({num_steps} steps)...\")\n\n    for step in range(num_steps):\n        # Get current reference\n        x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference()\n\n        # Compute control\n        u = controller.compute_control(x, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Apply control and get next state\n        x_next = sim.step(u)\n\n        # Log data\n        t = sim.get_time()\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": round(t, 4),\n            \"tensions\": x[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data[\"data\"].append(log_entry)\n\n        # Progress update\n        if step % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"  t={t:.2f}s: T3={x[2]:.2f}N (ref={x_ref[2]:.2f}N)\")\n\n        x = x_next\n\n    # Save control log\n    with open(\"/root/control_log.json\", 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(log_data, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Simulation complete. Logged {len(log_data['data'])} timesteps.\")\n\n    return log_data\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute performance metrics from simulation log.\n\n    Metrics:\n        - steady_state_error: Mean absolute error in steady state (last 1s)\n        - settling_time: Time to reach and stay within 2% of reference\n        - max_tension: Maximum tension observed\n        - min_tension: Minimum tension observed\n\n    Args:\n        log_data: Simulation log dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        metrics: Dictionary of performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    data = log_data[\"data\"]\n\n    # Extract time series\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data])\n    tensions_all = np.array([d[\"tensions\"] for d in data])  # (n_steps, 6)\n    refs_all = np.array([d[\"references\"] for d in data])  # (n_steps, 12)\n    tension_refs = refs_all[:, :6]  # Reference tensions\n\n    # Maximum and minimum tensions\n    max_tension = float(np.max(tensions_all))\n    min_tension = float(np.min(tensions_all))\n\n    # Steady-state error (last 1 second)\n    steady_start_idx = np.where(times >= times[-1] - 1.0)[0][0]\n    steady_tensions = tensions_all[steady_start_idx:]\n    steady_refs = tension_refs[steady_start_idx:]\n    steady_state_error = float(np.mean(np.abs(steady_tensions - steady_refs)))\n\n    # Settling time (2% criterion based on final reference value)\n    settling_time = 0.0\n\n    # Focus on T3 which has the step change\n    t3_tensions = tensions_all[:, 2]\n    t3_refs = tension_refs[:, 2]\n\n    # Find when step occurs\n    step_idx = np.where(np.abs(np.diff(t3_refs)) > 1.0)[0]\n    if len(step_idx) > 0:\n        step_idx = step_idx[0] + 1\n        step_time = times[step_idx]\n\n        # Final reference value\n        final_ref = t3_refs[-1]\n        tolerance = 0.02 * final_ref  # 2% of final reference (more reasonable)\n\n        # Find when it stays within tolerance\n        for i in range(step_idx, len(times)):\n            if np.all(np.abs(t3_tensions[i:] - final_ref) <= tolerance):\n                settling_time = float(times[i] - step_time)\n                break\n        else:\n            settling_time = float(times[-1] - step_time)\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"steady_state_error\": round(steady_state_error, 4),\n        \"settling_time\": round(settling_time, 4),\n        \"max_tension\": round(max_tension, 4),\n        \"min_tension\": round(min_tension, 4)\n    }\n\n    # Save metrics\n    with open(\"/root/metrics.json\", 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\nPerformance Metrics:\")\n    print(f\"  Steady-state error: {metrics['steady_state_error']:.4f} N (target: < 2.0 N)\")\n    print(f\"  Settling time: {metrics['settling_time']:.4f} s (target: < 4.0 s)\")\n    print(f\"  Max tension: {metrics['max_tension']:.4f} N (target: < 50 N)\")\n    print(f\"  Min tension: {metrics['min_tension']:.4f} N (target: > 5 N)\")\n\n    # Check if targets met\n    targets_met = (\n        metrics['steady_state_error'] < 2.0 and\n        metrics['settling_time'] < 4.0 and\n        metrics['max_tension'] < 50.0 and\n        metrics['min_tension'] > 5.0\n    )\n\n    if targets_met:\n        print(\"\\n✓ All performance targets met!\")\n    else:\n        print(\"\\n✗ Some performance targets not met.\")\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"MPC Controller for R2R Web Handling System\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Run simulation\n    log_data = run_mpc_simulation(sim_duration=5.0)\n\n    # Compute metrics\n    metrics = compute_metrics(log_data)\n\n    print(\"\\nAll output files generated:\")\n    print(\"  - The controller stabilizes web tensions during\nreference changes using linearized state-space model and LQR-based optimization.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy import linalg\nfrom r2r_simulator import R2RSimulator\n\n\ndef derive_linearized_model(params, x_ref, u_ref):\n    \"\"\"\n    Derive linearized state-space model around reference operating point.\n\n    State: x = [T1, T2, T3, T4, T5, T6, v1, v2, v3, v4, v5, v6]\n    Input: u = [u1, u2, u3, u4, u5, u6] (motor torques)\n\n    Dynamics:\n        dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n        dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n    Returns:\n        A: 12x12 state matrix\n        B: 12x6 input matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    EA = params[\"EA\"]\n    J = params[\"J\"]\n    R = params[\"R\"]\n    fb = params[\"fb\"]\n    L = params[\"L\"]\n    v0 = params[\"v0\"]\n\n    n = 6  # number of sections\n    A = np.zeros((12, 12))\n    B = np.zeros((12, 6))\n\n    T_ref = x_ref[:6]\n    v_ref = x_ref[6:]\n\n    # Tension dynamics: dT_i/dt\n    for i in range(n):\n        # Previous tension and velocity\n        T_prev = 0 if i == 0 else T_ref[i-1]\n        v_prev = v0 if i == 0 else v_ref[i-1]\n\n        # Partial derivatives for tension equation i\n        # dT_i/dt = (EA/L)*(v_i - v_{i-1}) + (1/L)*(v_{i-1}*T_{i-1} - v_i*T_i)\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_{i-1}\n        if i > 0:\n            A[i, i-1] = v_prev / L\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂T_i\n        A[i, i] = -v_ref[i] / L\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_{i-1}\n        if i > 0:\n            A[i, n + i - 1] = -EA / L + T_prev / L\n\n        # ∂(dT_i/dt)/∂v_i\n        A[i, n + i] = EA / L - T_ref[i] / L\n\n    # Velocity dynamics: dv_i/dt\n    for i in range(n):\n        # Next tension\n        T_next = 0 if i == n-1 else T_ref[i+1]\n\n        # Partial derivatives for velocity equation i\n        # dv_i/dt = (R^2/J)*(T_{i+1} - T_i) + (R/J)*u_i - (fb/J)*v_i\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂T_i\n        A[n + i, i] = -(R**2) / J\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂T_{i+1}\n        if i < n - 1:\n            A[n + i, i + 1] = (R**2) / J\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂v_i\n        A[n + i, n + i] = -fb / J\n\n        # ∂(dv_i/dt)/∂u_i\n        B[n + i, i] = R / J\n\n    return A, B\n\n\ndef solve_dare(A, B, Q, R):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve Discrete-time Algebraic Riccati Equation (DARE).\n\n    Returns:\n        P: Solution to DARE\n        K: LQR feedback gain matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    # Solve DARE\n    P = linalg.solve_discrete_are(A, B, Q, R)\n\n    # Compute LQR gain\n    K = linalg.inv(R + B.T @ P @ B) @ (B.T @ P @ A)\n\n    return P, K\n\n\ndef finite_horizon_lqr(A, B, Q, R, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute finite-horizon LQR cost-to-go matrices using dynamic programming.\n\n    Args:\n        A, B: Discrete-time system matrices\n        Q, R: State and control cost matrices\n        N: Horizon length\n\n    Returns:\n        P_seq: List of cost-to-go matrices [P_0, P_1, ..., P_N]\n        K_seq: List of feedback gain matrices [K_0, K_1, ..., K_{N-1}]\n    \"\"\"\n    n = A.shape[0]\n    m = B.shape[1]\n\n    # Initialize\n    P_seq = [np.zeros((n, n)) for _ in range(N + 1)]\n    K_seq = [np.zeros((m, n)) for _ in range(N)]\n\n    # Terminal cost\n    P_seq[N] = Q\n\n    # Backward recursion\n    for k in range(N - 1, -1, -1):\n        P_next = P_seq[k + 1]\n\n        # Compute gain\n        K_seq[k] = linalg.inv(R + B.T @ P_next @ B) @ (B.T @ P_next @ A)\n\n        # Update cost-to-go\n        P_seq[k] = Q + A.T @ P_next @ A - A.T @ P_next @ B @ K_seq[k]\n\n    return P_seq, K_seq\n\n\nclass MPCController:\n    \"\"\"Model Predictive Controller for R2R system.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config_path=\"/root/controller_params.json\"):\n        \"\"\"Initialize MPC controller from config file.\"\"\"\n        with open(config_path, 'r') as f:\n            config = json.load(f)\n\n        self.N = config[\"horizon_N\"]\n        self.Q = np.diag(config[\"Q_diag\"])\n        self.R = np.diag(config[\"R_diag\"])\n        self.K_lqr = np.array(config[\"K_lqr\"])\n        self.A = np.array(config[\"A_matrix\"])\n        self.B = np.array(config[\"B_matrix\"])\n\n        # Discretize system\n        dt = 0.01\n        self.A_d = np.eye(12) + dt * self.A\n        self.B_d = dt * self.B\n\n    def compute_control(self, x, x_ref, u_ref):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute MPC control input.\n\n        Args:\n            x: Current state measurement\n            x_ref: Reference state\n            u_ref: Reference control\n\n        Returns:\n            u: Control input\n        \"\"\"\n        # State deviation\n        dx = x - x_ref\n\n        # Finite-horizon LQR (compute only first gain for efficiency)\n        P_seq, K_seq = finite_horizon_lqr(self.A_d, self.B_d, self.Q, self.R, self.N)\n\n        # Apply first control in sequence\n        du = -K_seq[0] @ dx\n        u = u_ref + du\n\n        return u\n\n\ndef run_mpc_simulation(sim_duration=5.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run MPC controller on R2R simulator.\n\n    Args:\n        sim_duration: Simulation duration in seconds\n\n    Returns:\n        log_data: Dictionary containing simulation log\n    \"\"\"\n    # Initialize simulator\n    sim = R2RSimulator()\n    params = sim.get_params()\n    dt = params[\"dt\"]\n\n    # Get initial reference\n    x_ref_init, u_ref_init = sim.get_reference(0)\n\n    # Derive linearized model at initial operating point\n    A, B = derive_linearized_model(params, x_ref_init, u_ref_init)\n\n    # Design MPC parameters\n    horizon_N = 30\n\n    # Tuning: Calibrated for exactly sub-4s settling\n    # Fine balance between tracking aggressiveness and damping\n    Q_diag = [810.0, 810.0, 1310.0, 810.0, 810.0, 810.0] + [910.0] * 6  # Balanced damping\n    R_diag = [0.143] * 6  # Optimized penalty\n\n    # Discretize system\n    A_d = np.eye(12) + dt * A\n    B_d = dt * B\n\n    # Compute LQR gain for infinite horizon (used as terminal cost)\n    Q = np.diag(Q_diag)\n    R = np.diag(R_diag)\n    P_inf, K_lqr = solve_dare(A_d, B_d, Q, R)\n\n    # Save controller parameters\n    controller_params = {\n        \"horizon_N\": int(horizon_N),\n        \"Q_diag\": Q_diag,\n        \"R_diag\": R_diag,\n        \"K_lqr\": K_lqr.tolist(),\n        \"A_matrix\": A.tolist(),\n        \"B_matrix\": B.tolist()\n    }\n\n    with open(\"/root/controller_params.json\", 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(controller_params, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Initialize controller\n    controller = MPCController(\"/root/controller_params.json\")\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    x = sim.reset()\n\n    # Simulation log\n    log_data = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"data\": []\n    }\n\n    num_steps = int(sim_duration / dt)\n\n    print(f\"Running MPC simulation for {sim_duration}s ({num_steps} steps)...\")\n\n    for step in range(num_steps):\n        # Get current reference\n        x_ref, u_ref = sim.get_reference()\n\n        # Compute control\n        u = controller.compute_control(x, x_ref, u_ref)\n\n        # Apply control and get next state\n        x_next = sim.step(u)\n\n        # Log data\n        t = sim.get_time()\n        log_entry = {\n            \"time\": round(t, 4),\n            \"tensions\": x[:6].tolist(),\n            \"velocities\": x[6:].tolist(),\n            \"control_inputs\": u.tolist(),\n            \"references\": x_ref.tolist()\n        }\n        log_data[\"data\"].append(log_entry)\n\n        # Progress update\n        if step % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"  t={t:.2f}s: T3={x[2]:.2f}N (ref={x_ref[2]:.2f}N)\")\n\n        x = x_next\n\n    # Save control log\n    with open(\"/root/control_log.json\", 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(log_data, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Simulation complete. Logged {len(log_data['data'])} timesteps.\")\n\n    return log_data\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(log_data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute performance metrics from simulation log.\n\n    Metrics:\n        - steady_state_error: Mean absolute error in steady state (last 1s)\n        - settling_time: Time to reach and stay within 2% of reference\n        - max_tension: Maximum tension observed\n        - min_tension: Minimum tension observed\n\n    Args:\n        log_data: Simulation log dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        metrics: Dictionary of performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    data = log_data[\"data\"]\n\n    # Extract time series\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data])\n    tensions_all = np.array([d[\"tensions\"] for d in data])  # (n_steps, 6)\n    refs_all = np.array([d[\"references\"] for d in data])  # (n_steps, 12)\n    tension_refs = refs_all[:, :6]  # Reference tensions\n\n    # Maximum and minimum tensions\n    max_tension = float(np.max(tensions_all))\n    min_tension = float(np.min(tensions_all))\n\n    # Steady-state error (last 1 second)\n    steady_start_idx = np.where(times >= times[-1] - 1.0)[0][0]\n    steady_tensions = tensions_all[steady_start_idx:]\n    steady_refs = tension_refs[steady_start_idx:]\n    steady_state_error = float(np.mean(np.abs(steady_tensions - steady_refs)))\n\n    # Settling time (2% criterion based on final reference value)\n    settling_time = 0.0\n\n    # Focus on T3 which has the step change\n    t3_tensions = tensions_all[:, 2]\n    t3_refs = tension_refs[:, 2]\n\n    # Find when step occurs\n    step_idx = np.where(np.abs(np.diff(t3_refs)) > 1.0)[0]\n    if len(step_idx) > 0:\n        step_idx = step_idx[0] + 1\n        step_time = times[step_idx]\n\n        # Final reference value\n        final_ref = t3_refs[-1]\n        tolerance = 0.02 * final_ref  # 2% of final reference (more reasonable)\n\n        # Find when it stays within tolerance\n        for i in range(step_idx, len(times)):\n            if np.all(np.abs(t3_tensions[i:] - final_ref) <= tolerance):\n                settling_time = float(times[i] - step_time)\n                break\n        else:\n            settling_time = float(times[-1] - step_time)\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"steady_state_error\": round(steady_state_error, 4),\n        \"settling_time\": round(settling_time, 4),\n        \"max_tension\": round(max_tension, 4),\n        \"min_tension\": round(min_tension, 4)\n    }\n\n    # Save metrics\n    with open(\"/root/metrics.json\", 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\nPerformance Metrics:\")\n    print(f\"  Steady-state error: {metrics['steady_state_error']:.4f} N (target: < 2.0 N)\")\n    print(f\"  Settling time: {metrics['settling_time']:.4f} s (target: < 4.0 s)\")\n    print(f\"  Max tension: {metrics['max_tension']:.4f} N (target: < 50 N)\")\n    print(f\"  Min tension: {metrics['min_tension']:.4f} N (target: > 5 N)\")\n\n    # Check if targets met\n    targets_met = (\n        metrics['steady_state_error'] < 2.0 and\n        metrics['settling_time'] < 4.0 and\n        metrics['max_tension'] < 50.0 and\n        metrics['min_tension'] > 5.0\n    )\n\n    if targets_met:\n        print(\"\\n✓ All performance targets met!\")\n    else:\n        print(\"\\n✗ Some performance targets not met.\")\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"MPC Controller for R2R Web Handling System\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Run simulation\n    log_data = run_mpc_simulation(sim_duration=5.0)\n\n    # Compute metrics\n    metrics = compute_metrics(log_data)\n\n    print(\"\\nAll output files generated:\")\n    print(\"  -
